# ROADMAP 130: length give-aways in Spanish B1/B2 choice exercises — worksheet
#
# One entry per exercise. Identical copies (es-es + es-latam, lesson +
# consolidation) are ONE entry; `files:` lists every copy, and every copy
# gets the same change.
#
# Fill in `decision:` for EVERY entry in a block BEFORE you edit that block,
# with one of:
#   shorten: <the new correct option, in full>
#   match: <each new wrong option, in full, separated by " || ">
#   log                -> you are unsure; add it to es-review-questions.txt, change nothing
# then one short line `why:` saying why the result has exactly one right
# answer. An empty decision means the entry was not reviewed. The reviewer
# reads this file and diffs every changed option against it.


########## BLOCK: es-both b1 (127 exercises) ##########

### b1-01-05.ex06   teaches: ['pluscuamperfecto']
files: content/es-es/exercises/b1/b1-01-05-ex.json, content/es-latam/exercises/b1/b1-01-05-ex.json
Clara: ¿Por qué atacó Don Quijote los molinos?
Tú: _____
Clara: Claro, por eso los vio como gigantes.
- Porque los molinos lo atacaron primero.
* Porque había leído tantas historias de caballeros que imaginaba aventuras en todas partes.
- Porque leerá historias de caballeros.
decision: match: Porque creía que los campesinos del lugar corrían peligro cerca de las máquinas. || Porque pensaba que el dueño de las tierras le había robado su caballo esa noche.
why: Dialogue requires matching full past-tense explanations; removes tense clash and matches length.

### b1-02-05.ex13   teaches: ['pluscuamperfecto']
files: content/es-es/exercises/b1/b1-02-05-ex.json, content/es-latam/exercises/b1/b1-02-05-ex.json
El relato lo conmovió porque mostraba cómo una decisión pequeña podía cambiar una vida.
* The tale moved him because a small decision could change a life.
- The tale will change tomorrow.
- The tale was only about a place.
decision: match: The tale confused him because a small decision could not affect other people. || The tale bored him because the story only showed daily life in a quiet place.
why: Translation item requires full sentence; wrong options match length and avoid tense clash.

### b1-03-05.ex12   teaches: None
files: content/es-es/exercises/b1/b1-03-05-ex.json, content/es-latam/exercises/b1/b1-03-05-ex.json
¿Por qué algunas personas dudaban de Fogg?
* Porque creían que no conseguiría terminar el recorrido dentro del plazo.
- Porque no conocía ningún destino.
- Porque no tenía intención de viajar.
- Porque no había tomado una decisión.
decision: shorten: Porque creían que no terminaría a tiempo.
why: Reading comprehension; cuts verbose clause to key point matching distractor length.

### b1-04-04.ex02   teaches: ['porque-y-por-eso']
files: content/es-es/exercises/b1/b1-04-04-ex.json, content/es-latam/exercises/b1/b1-04-04-ex.json
Which sentence gives advice and explains a consequence? [¿Cuál da un consejo y explica una consecuencia?]
* Deberías hablar con él porque así evitarás un problema mayor. [You should speak to him because that way you will avoid a bigger problem.]
- Habla con él. [Speak to him.]
- Hablaste con él en la reunión. [You spoke to him in the meeting.]
decision: match: Deberías hablar con él mañana por la mañana en la sala de reuniones. [You should speak to him tomorrow morning in the meeting room.] || Tuviste un problema mayor con él porque no hablaste a tiempo en la reunión. [You had a bigger problem with him because you did not speak in time in the meeting.]
why: Advice plus consequence item; wrong options match length using advice alone and consequence alone.

### b1-04-05.ex16   teaches: ['condicional-consejos']
files: content/es-es/exercises/b1/b1-04-05-ex.json, content/es-latam/exercises/b1/b1-04-05-ex.json
Which recommendation is the most complete? [¿Cuál recomendación está más completa?]
* Te recomiendo que revises el plan para que puedas evitar un riesgo innecesario. [I recommend that you review the plan so that you can avoid an unnecessary risk.]
- Revisa el plan. [Review the plan.]
- Tienes que revisar el plan. [You have to review the plan.]
decision: match: Revisa el plan esta tarde en la oficina con tus compañeros. [Review the plan this afternoon in the office with your colleagues.] || Tienes que revisar el plan con el equipo durante la reunión de esta tarde. [You have to review the plan with the team during this afternoon's meeting.]
why: Complete recommendation item; wrong options match length using direct imperative and obligation.

### b1-06-05.ex02   teaches: ['b1-work-professional-life-vocab']
files: content/es-es/exercises/b1/b1-06-05-ex.json, content/es-latam/exercises/b1/b1-06-05-ex.json
Which is the clearest professional refusal? [¿Cuál es la negativa profesional más clara?]
* No puedo aceptar el cambio de plazo porque tengo que terminar otro proyecto. [I cannot accept the change to the deadline because I have to finish another project.]
- No. [No.]
- No quiero. [I don't want to.]
decision: match: No voy a hacerlo porque no me apetece asumir más responsabilidades ahora. [I am not going to do it because I don't feel like taking on more responsibilities now.] || No quiero ese cambio porque simplemente no me parece una buena idea. [I don't want that change because it simply doesn't seem like a good idea to me.]
why: Professional refusal item; wrong options match length while remaining informal and unprofessional.

* Creo que la práctica es esencial porque permite aplicar lo aprendido. [I think practice is essential because it allows you to apply what you have learned.]
- Creo que sí. [I think so.]
- No sé. [I don't know.]
decision: match: Creo que es un tema interesante, pero prefiero no hablar de mis preferencias. [I think it is an interesting topic, but I prefer not to talk about my preferences.] || La práctica dura dos horas todos los días según el horario oficial del curso. [Practice lasts two hours every day according to the official schedule of the course.]

* Because the delay and breakdown made the original plan impossible. [Porque el retraso y la avería hicieron imposible el plan original.]
- Because he wanted to leave Ithaca. [Porque quería salir de Ítaca.]
- Because he had no destination. [Porque no tenía destino.]
decision: shorten: Because the breakdown ruined the original plan. [Porque la avería arruinó el plan original.]
why: Reading comprehension; shortens to key cause matching distractor style and length.

### b1-10-consolidation.ex15   teaches: ['hypothetical-structures']

Which description combines evaluation and reason? [¿Cuál combina una valoración y una razón?]
* Prefiero una vivienda funcional porque necesito espacio para trabajar. [I prefer a functional home because I need space to work.]
- Prefiero una vivienda funcional. [I prefer a functional home.]
- La vivienda tiene dos habitaciones. [The home has two bedrooms.]
decision: match: Prefiero vivir en una casa amplia y luminosa situada cerca del centro histórico. [I prefer living in a bright and spacious house located near the historic centre.] || La vivienda tiene tres habitaciones exteriores, dos baños completos y una terraza grande. [The home has three exterior bedrooms, two full bathrooms and a large terrace.]

* Estoy de acuerdo con la propuesta, aunque creo que necesitamos más información. [I agree with the proposal, although I think we need more information.]
- Sí. [Yes.]
- No sé. [I don't know.]
decision: match: Sí, me parece bien todo lo que han propuesto y no tengo nada más que añadir ahora. [Yes, everything they proposed seems fine to me and I have nothing more to add now.] || No sé qué decir sobre el asunto porque todavía no he pensado en ninguna solución. [I don't know what to say about the matter because I haven't thought of any solution yet.]
why: Moving discussion forward item; wrong options match length while explicitly not contributing further.

### b1-12-04.ex02   teaches: ['contrast-concession-connectors']
files: content/es-es/exercises/b1/b1-12-04-ex.json, content/es-latam/exercises/b1/b1-12-04-ex.json
Which sentence makes a comparison? [¿Cuál hace una comparación?]
* La comida casera suele ser más barata que comer fuera. [Homemade food is usually cheaper than eating out.]
- La comida casera es barata. [Homemade food is cheap.]
- Como comida casera. [I eat homemade food.]
decision: match: La comida casera suele ser bastante saludable para toda la familia. [Homemade food is usually quite healthy for the whole family.] || Preparo comida casera todas las noches después de volver del trabajo. [I prepare homemade food every night after returning from work.]
why: Comparative grammar item; wrong options match length without containing a comparative structure.

### b1-12-05.ex02   teaches: ['preterite-vs-imperfect']
files: content/es-es/exercises/b1/b1-12-05-ex.json, content/es-latam/exercises/b1/b1-12-05-ex.json
Which sentence describes a change over time? [¿Cuál describe un cambio con el tiempo?]
* Con el tiempo aprendí a preparar platos más equilibrados. [Over time I learned to prepare more balanced dishes.]
- Siempre cocino lo mismo. [I always cook the same thing.]
- Hoy preparo la cena. [Today I prepare dinner.]
decision: match: Todos los fines de semana cocino los mismos platos tradicionales en casa. [Every weekend I cook the same traditional dishes at home.] || Hoy preparo una cena especial para celebrar el cumpleaños de mi hermano. [Today I prepare a special dinner to celebrate my brother's birthday.]
why: Change over time item; wrong options match length using habitual and single-event sentences.

### b1-12-consolidation.ex02   teaches: ['frequency-adverbs']
files: content/es-es/exercises/b1/b1-12-consolidation-ex.json, content/es-latam/exercises/b1/b1-12-consolidation-ex.json
Which sentence combines a habit with a contrast? [¿Cuál combina un hábito con un contraste?]
* Normalmente desayuno temprano, aunque los fines de semana como más tarde. [I normally have breakfast early, although at weekends I eat later.]
- Normalmente desayuno temprano. [I normally have breakfast early.]
- Los fines de semana como más tarde. [At weekends I eat later.]
decision: match: Normalmente desayuno temprano en la cocina antes de salir para la oficina. [I normally have breakfast early in the kitchen before leaving for the office.] || Los fines de semana siempre como más tarde porque no tengo que madrugar. [At weekends I always eat later because I don't have to get up early.]
why: Habit plus contrast item; wrong options match length using habit alone and cause alone.

### b1-12-consolidation.ex16   teaches: ['preterite-vs-imperfect']
files: content/es-es/exercises/b1/b1-12-consolidation-ex.json, content/es-latam/exercises/b1/b1-12-consolidation-ex.json
Which sentence combines comparison and contrast? [¿Cuál combina comparación y contraste?]
* Comer en casa suele ser más barato que comer fuera, aunque requiere más tiempo. [Eating at home is usually cheaper than eating out, although it requires more time.]
- Comer en casa suele ser barato. [Eating at home is usually cheap.]
- Comer fuera requiere tiempo. [Eating out requires time.]
decision: match: Comer en casa suele ser mucho más barato que salir a almorzar a un restaurante. [Eating at home is usually much cheaper than going out to lunch at a restaurant.] || Comer en casa resulta muy saludable, aunque requiere bastante tiempo preparar todo. [Eating at home is very healthy, although it requires quite a lot of time to prepare everything.]
why: Comparison plus contrast item; wrong options match length with comparison alone and contrast alone.

### b1-13-01.ex02   teaches: ['estilo-indirecto-informacion']

* Según la fuente oficial [According to the official source]
- En mi opinión [In my opinion]
- Tal vez [Perhaps]
decision: match: Desde mi punto de vista [From my point of view] || A mi modo de ver [In my way of looking at it]
why: Source marker question; wrong options match length using personal-opinion markers.

### b1-13-consolidation.ex02   teaches: ['estilo-indirecto-informacion']
files: content/es-es/exercises/b1/b1-13-consolidation-ex.json, content/es-latam/exercises/b1/b1-13-consolidation-ex.json
Which sentence clearly reports information from a source? [¿Cuál informa claramente de una fuente?]
* Según la fuente oficial, el suceso ocurrió durante la noche. [According to the official source, the incident happened during the night.]
- Me parece extraño. [It seems strange to me.]
- No creo que sea posible. [I do not think it is possible.]
decision: match: A mí me parece muy extraño todo lo que explicaron los vecinos sobre el caso. [To me everything the neighbours explained about the case seems very strange.] || No creo que sea posible que ocurriera algo así sin que nadie pudiera verlo. [I do not think it is possible that something like that happened without anyone seeing it.]
why: Source-reporting function; wrong options match length while expressing personal reaction and doubt.

- Hay testimonios. [There are testimonies.]
- La fuente informó de un vídeo. [The source reported a video.]
decision: match: En resumen, los periodistas publicaron los primeros datos a primera hora del día. [In summary, the journalists published the initial details early in the day.] || La fuente oficial informó de la existencia de un vídeo grabado por varios vecinos. [The official source reported the existence of a video recorded by several neighbours.]
why: Summary plus evaluation item; wrong options match length without combining summary marker and evaluation.

### b1-15-04.ex02   teaches: ['relative-clauses']

### b1-15-consolidation.ex02   teaches: ['giving-opinions']
files: content/es-es/exercises/b1/b1-15-consolidation-ex.json, content/es-latam/exercises/b1/b1-15-consolidation-ex.json
Which sentence expresses a preference and a reason? [¿Cuál expresa una preferencia y una razón?]
* Prefiero las novelas porque puedo imaginar mejor a los personajes. [I prefer novels because I can imagine the characters better.]
- La novela tiene tres capítulos. [The novel has three chapters.]
- La película dura dos horas. [The film lasts two hours.]
decision: match: Esta novela clásica tiene más de cuatrocientos capítulos en total. [This classic novel has more than four hundred chapters in total.] || La película adaptada dura casi tres horas en la versión original. [The adapted film lasts almost three hours in the original version.]
why: Preference plus reason function; wrong options match length using factual statements without preference or reason.

### b1-15-consolidation.ex16   teaches: ['argument-markers']

- La película dura dos horas. [The film lasts two hours.]
